#386 Deoxys
------------
Ability:
Pressure [All Formes]
No change 

Stats:
[Normal Forme]
50/150/50/150/50/150
[Attack Forme]
50/180/20/180/20/150
[Defense Forme]
50/70/160/70/160/90
[Speed Forme]
50/95/90/95/90/180
No Changes

Deoxys has seperate learnsets depending on it's current form.
These are denoted by NADS (Normal/Attack/Defense/Speed)
and example would be  

Level up learnset:
1 - Wrap
1 - ThunderPunch
1 - Fire Punch
1 - Ice Punch
1 - Drain Punch
1 - Trick
1 - Stealth Rock
10 - Night Shade
20 - Knock Off
30 - Pursuit/Superpower/Spikes/Fake Out [NADS]
40 - Psyshock
50 - Zen Headbutt
60 - Rock Slide
65 - Cosmic Power
